The gradient noise is considered to have a finite $δ$-th moment ($1<δ\le 2$), which generalizes the Gaussian noise and covers cases with infinite variance. In this work, we employ the classic method Median-of-Means (MoM) in robust estimation. MoM works by dividing samples into blocks, taking the average of each block, and then taking the median of these block averages, achieving a breakdown point of up to $1/2$. This makes the final estimate reliable even when some samples are very noisy or wrong, and thus is effective to handle the heavy-tailed noise. The method also naturally defends against malicious gradient attacks. Compared with gradient clipping, which is the most popular method to deal with the heavy-tailed noise, MoM requires no preset clipping threshold and is insensitive to the tail behavior of the noise. Under standard assumptions, we prove the almost sure convergence of the algorithm and derive its almost sure convergence rate. To address the systematic bias caused by asymmetric noise, we further design an online bias correction strategy. Simulation results show the effectiveness and efficiency of the proposed algorithms.

</details>


### [119] [Robust Design of Multi-Energy Systems Accounting for Mixed-Integer Operational Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26613)
*Moritz Wedemeyer,Alexander Mitsos,Manuel Dahmen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文理论研究了可行性时间步启发式在非凸条件下识别多能源系统鲁棒设计的有效性，发现非凸性可能导致启发式失效，并提出了一种混合验证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多能源系统鲁棒设计的识别在计算上具有挑战性，严格方法通常计算不可行，因此需要借助启发式方法。然而启发式方法的鲁棒性缺乏理论保证，本文旨在从理论上探究常见非凸性对启发式方法的影响。

Method: 理论分析三种常见非凸性（分段线性能量流入-流出关系、最小部分负荷、储能互补性）对可行性时间步启发式的影响；提出一种混合方法，将整数变量离散化并将下层问题的对偶嵌入单层问题，以验证启发式识别设计的鲁棒性。

Result: 非凸性可能导致启发式失效；若允许弃能，储能互补性不再影响鲁棒性，凸分段线性关系可线性重构，但最小部分负荷仍可能导致失效；若目标函数在不确定变量和操作变量上非联合凸，启发式也可能失效；案例中最小部分负荷和目标函数对热泵效率的非凸依赖被识别为可能的失效模式。

Conclusion: 可行性时间步启发式在存在非凸性时仍可能识别鲁棒设计，但成功与否取决于具体问题；该启发式可作为初步设计识别步骤，但需要鲁棒性保证时必须采用严格方法。

Abstract: Identifying robust designs for multi-energy systems is computationally challenging. As rigorous approaches are often computationally intractable, heuristics are employed to generate candidate designs. One example is the feasibility time-step heuristic by Teichgraeber et al. [Appl. Energy, 275, 115223, 2020]. We theoretically investigate how 3 common nonconvexities, i.e., piecewise-linear energy inflow-outflow relationships, minimal part-loads, and storage complementarity, affect the robustness of designs identified by this heuristic. We find that, if surplus energy cannot be curtailed, any of these nonconvexities may cause the heuristic to fail. If curtailment is allowed, storage complementarity does not compromise robustness, and convex piecewise-linear inflow-outflow relationships can be reformulated linearly. However, minimal part loads may lead to failure of the heuristic. Furthermore, if the objective function of the operational problem is not jointly convex in the uncertain variables and the operational variables, the heuristic may fail. We consider an illustrative multi-energy system, where minimal part-loads and nonconvex dependence of the objective function on heat-pump efficiency are identified as possible failure modes. We propose a hybrid method that discretizes integer variables and embeds the dual of the lower-level problem into a single-level formulation to verify whether a design identified by the heuristic is robust. The results show that the feasibility time-step heuristic may identify robust designs despite the presence of nonconvexities, but the success is case-dependent. Hence, the heuristic can serve as a first step in identifying robust designs; however, when robustness guarantees are required, rigorous methods are necessary.

</details>


### [120] [Quasar-Convex Optimization: Fundamental Properties and High-Order Proximal-Point Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26735)
*Masoud Ahookhosh,Jose M. M. de Brito,Alireza Kabgani,Felipe Lara,Jinyun Yuan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了（强）拟凸函数的优化问题，提出了高阶正则化的近端点算法（HiPPA），并分析了不同正则化阶数 p 下的收敛行为：p∈(1,2) 局部线性收敛，p=2 全局线性收敛，p>2 超线性收敛，复杂度分别为 O(log(1/ε)) 和 O(log log(1/ε))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟凸函数在机器学习和数据科学中自然出现且具有良好性质，但其优化理论尚不完善，需要系统性的算法设计和收敛分析。

Method: 首先建立了拟凸函数的基本性质（稳定性、增长条件、无虚假临界点），然后提出高阶正则化近端点算法（HiPPA），并基于正则化阶数 p 进行统一的收敛性分析。

Result: 揭示了关于阶数 p 的锐利相变行为：p∈(1,2) 局部线性收敛（复杂度 O(log(1/ε))），p=2 全局线性收敛（相同复杂度），p>2 超线性收敛（复杂度 O(log log(1/ε))）。数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: HiPPA 算法在不同正则化阶数下表现出不同的收敛特性，其中高阶正则化（p>2）可实现超线性收敛，为拟凸优化提供了高效的理论框架和实用算法。

Abstract: We study the optimization of (strongly) quasar-convex functions, a class that arises naturally in many machine learning and data science applications due to its favorable properties. The fundamental properties of this class are first developed, including its stability under standard calculus operations, growth conditions, and the absence of spurious critical points, which together imply a benign global geometry with no saddle points. Motivated by these properties, a class of proximal-point algorithms (HiPPA) with high-order regularization of order $p>1$ is introduced. Conditions are identified under which the iterates converge globally to minimizers, and a unified convergence analysis is provided with explicit rates and iteration complexity bounds under appropriate regularity assumptions. The results reveal a sharp transition in behavior with respect to the order $p$: for $p\in(1,2)$, the method achieves local linear convergence with complexity $\mathcal{O}(\log(\varepsilon^{-1}))$ when initialized sufficiently close to a minimizer; for $p=2$, it attains global linear convergence with the same complexity; and for $p>2$, it exhibits superlinear convergence with complexity $\mathcal{O}(\log\log(\varepsilon^{-1}))$, where $\varepsilon>0$ denotes the target accuracy. The theory is complemented with preliminary numerical experiments on selected machine learning problems, which illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods and are consistent with the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [121] [Induced Stackelberg Equilibrium Seeking via Iterative Tikhonov Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26746)
*Silvia Cianchi,Anibal Sanjab,Sergio Grammatico*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对下层博弈存在多个纳什均衡时Stackelberg学习算法可能失效的问题，本文通过引入最优均衡选择机制，让领导者添加一个渐消激励项来诱导特定均衡，并提出一种无需知晓跟随者信息的零阶优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Stackelberg均衡学习方法通常假设下层博弈（变分/广义）纳什均衡唯一，但多重均衡存在时若无选择约定，问题可能不适定，标准算法无法收敛。

Method: 领导者在下层博弈中引入一个渐消的额外激励项以诱导均衡选择，从而定义具有均衡选择诱导的Stackelberg博弈；提出一种跟随者无关的零阶方法，领导者通过迭代探测跟随者并联合更新决策变量与激励项来收敛到问题解。

Result: 所提方法能够在领导者不知晓跟随者模型的情况下，通过零阶优化收敛到具有均衡选择的Stackelberg博弈解。

Conclusion: 通过引入渐消激励项实现最优均衡选择，解决了多重纳什均衡下Stackelberg学习的不适定问题，为领导者提供了一种实用且收敛的求解框架。

Abstract: Existing methods for learning Stackelberg equilibria typically assume that the followers' (variational, generalized) Nash equilibrium is unique. However, in the presence of multiple equilibria, without a selection convention, the problem may become ill-posed, thus leading standard algorithms to potentially fail to converge. This paper addresses this issue by introducing an optimal selection at the lower-level game, hereby defining a Stackelberg game with induced equilibrium selection. To this end, we enable the leader to augment the followers' game with an additional vanishing term that acts as an incentive. We then propose a follower-agnostic zeroth-order method, whereby the leader converges to a solution of the resulting problem by iteratively probing the followers and jointly updating its decision variable and the incentive term.

</details>


### [122] [Function-free Optimization via Comparison Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26867)
*Katya Scheinberg,Zikai Xiong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究仅通过比较 oracle（给定两点，返回哪个更优）进行优化的问题，称为无函数优化。基于偏好水平集的几何结构，提出了水平集最优性间隙和正则半径等概念，并设计了法方向下降法，在凸性和正则性条件下达到近乎最优的样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际场景（如偏好排序、人机交互）中，目标函数及其梯度不可用或无意义，只能获得两两比较的偏好反馈。传统优化方法依赖显式目标函数，无法处理此类设置。本文旨在建立无需显式目标函数的优化理论框架。

Method: 1) 基于偏好水平集的几何分析框架，定义水平集最优性间隙和正则半径；2) 法方向估计：在 d 维欧氏空间中，使用 O(d log(d/ε)) 次比较达到 ε 精度（接近下界 Ω(d log(1/ε))）；3) 法方向下降法：结合法方向估计与步长更新，在凸性、正则性和局部增长条件下，总比较次数为 Õ(dD²/ε²)，步数为 O(D²/ε²)；4) 自适应方案：无需预先知道问题参数，复杂度与固定参数版本仅差对数因子。

Result: 法方向估计的样本复杂度上下界几乎匹配；法方向下降法的步数匹配法方向跨度方法的下界 Ω(D²/ε²)；自适应算法达到与固定参数版本相同的复杂度（对数因子内）。

Conclusion: 本文建立了仅依赖比较 oracle 的优化理论框架，证明了在几何正则性条件下，该方法在样本复杂度上几乎最优，为无函数优化提供了理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: In this work, we study optimization specified only through a comparison oracle: given two points, it reports which one is preferred. We call it function-free optimization because we do not assume access to, nor the existence of, a canonical application-given objective function. The goal is to find the most preferred feasible point, which we call the optimal solution. This model arises in preference- and ranking-based settings where objective values and derivatives are unavailable or meaningless. Even when a representative function exists, it may be nonsmooth, nonconvex, or discontinuous. We develop an analytical and algorithmic framework based on the geometry of preference level sets, which remains well-defined from comparisons alone. We introduce the level-set optimality gap, the distance from a preference level set to the optimal solutions, and the regularity radius, a stationarity certificate. Under regularity of the preference relation in a $d$-dimensional Euclidean space, we estimate normal directions to accuracy $ε$ using $O(d\log(d/ε))$ comparisons, nearly matching a lower bound of $Ω(d\log(1/ε))$. Under convexity, regularity, and a local growth condition on the regularity radius, the resulting normal direction descent method reaches an $ε$ level-set optimality gap using at most $\widetilde O(dD^2/ε^2)$ comparisons over $O(D^2/ε^2)$ normal direction estimation steps, where $D$ is the distance from the initial point to the optimal solutions. This number of steps matches the lower bound of $Ω(D^2/ε^2)$ for normal direction span-based methods. Since prior knowledge in practical applications is usually limited, we also develop adaptive schemes for estimating the normal direction and solving the optimization problem. They match the fixed-parameter complexity bounds up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [123] [Generalization of Zeroth-Order Method for Quotients of Quadratic Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26913)
*Jonas Bresch*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无约束球面采样的随机搜索方法，用于计算广义算子范数和极值广义瑞利商，并与Riemannian零阶优化方法建立联系，通过替代估计计算Riemannian梯度和Hessian，从而构建出加速算法，达到state-of-the-art性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在子空间和迭代优化方法中，二次函数及其商的最优化问题具有重要意义。现有方法存在约束采样的局限性，且缺乏与Riemannian优化方法的系统联系。本文旨在通过无约束球面采样的方式，更高效地计算广义算子范数和极值广义瑞利商。

Method: 1) 提出无约束的整个球面随机搜索方向采样策略；2) 将方法与Riemannian一阶和二阶零阶优化方法建立联系，通过特定替代估计Riemannian梯度和Hessian；3) 在不需要切空间的情况下，推导闭式最优步长；4) 将子问题阐明为特定Gram矩阵上的子广义瑞利商问题。

Result: 构建的加速算法表现出state-of-the-art的行为，并优于近期相关工作。

Conclusion: 本文通过无约束球面采样策略和与Riemannian零阶优化的理论联系，提供了更高效的广义瑞利商计算方法，所提出的加速算法在性能上超越了现有方法。

Abstract: Optimization of quadratic functions and the quotient of those are relevant in subspace and iterative optimization methods. In this paper, the calculation of the generalized operator norm and extremal generalized Rayleigh quotient is considered. In contrast to recent works an unconstrained sampling approach on the entire sphere for the random search direction in each iteration is proposed. Furthermore, the link to zeroth-order methods for Riemannian first- and second-order optimization methods is provided in the sense that the Riemannian gradient and Hessian are estimated by the specific surrogates. Even though the tangent space is not used in this construction the optimal step size problem can be computed in a closed form. The subproblems of this and recent works are illuminated in the context of sub-generalized Rayleigh quotient problems on specific Gram matrices. Together the achieved theory allows to construct an accelerated algorithm which shows state-of-the-art behavior and outperforms recent works.

</details>


### [124] [Learning Over-Relaxation Policies for ADMM with Convergence Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26932)
*Junan Lin,Paul J. Goulart,Luca Furieri*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出在ADMM中使用在线学习更新松弛参数的方法，在不触发矩阵重分解的前提下提升特定问题类（如MPC）的性能，并在二次规划基准测试中验证了收敛性和计算效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ADMM中惩罚参数和松弛参数的选择对实际性能影响很大，而在模型预测控制等场景中需要反复求解结构固定但参数值变化的优化问题，因此希望学习在线更新松弛参数以提升性能。

Method: 在OSQP类架构中，利用松弛参数更新无需矩阵重分解的计算优势，设计在线学习策略来动态调整松弛参数，同时建立时变惩罚和松弛参数下ADMM的收敛性保证。

Result: 在基准二次规划上，学习得到的策略相比基线OSQP在迭代次数和实际运行时间上均有提升。

Conclusion: 在线学习松弛参数是一种计算高效且可收敛的ADMM加速方法，适用于结构固定的重复优化问题。

Abstract: The Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) is a widely used method for structured convex optimization, and its practical performance depends strongly on the choice of penalty and relaxation parameters. Motivated by settings such as Model Predictive Control (MPC), where one repeatedly solves related optimization problems with fixed structure and changing parameter values, we propose learning online updates of the relaxation parameter to improve performance on problem classes of interest. This choice is computationally attractive in OSQP-like architectures, since adapting relaxation does not trigger the matrix refactorizations associated with penalty updates. We establish convergence guarantees for ADMM with time-varying penalty and relaxation parameters under mild assumptions, and show on benchmark quadratic programs that the resulting learned policies improve both iteration count and wall-clock time over baseline OSQP.

</details>
